{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CehnS27TsEfpwVeF8gMPv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013LYVVjUeDBUtCadkhM3AqE","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/nbody_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r2/nbody/nbody.cpp","content":"#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// Number of bodies whose force accumulation is interleaved in the inner loop.\n// Each of the LANES accumulators sums over j in ascending order exactly as the\n// scalar version does, so results are bit-identical while the compiler is free\n// to place the lanes into SIMD registers.\nconstexpr int LANES = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Block decomposition of [0, n) over `size` ranks: the first (n % size) ranks\n// get one extra element.\nstruct Partition {\n    std::vector<int> counts; // number of bodies owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> displs; // first body index owned by each rank\n};\n\nstatic Partition partitionBodies(const int n, const int size) {\n    Partition p;\n    p.counts.resize(size);\n    p.displs.resize(size);\n    const int base = n / size;\n    const int rem = n % size;\n    int off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        p.counts[r] = base + (r < rem ? 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    int l = 0;\n\n    for (; l + LANES <= localN; l += LANES) {\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i; work per i shrinks with i, so\n    // the two halves of the index space are paired up for load balance.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1], pz = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        const double vx = vel[3 * l + 0], vy = vel[3 * l + 1], vz = vel[3 * l + 2];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) || !std::isfinite(pz) || !std::isfinite(vx) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy) || !std::isfinite(vz)) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 1;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, const int rank,\n                        MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int firstBad = 0, reason = 0;\n    validateLocal(pos, begin, localN, vel, firstBad, reason);\n\n    // Report the violation of the lowest-numbered body, as the serial code does.\n    int in[2] = {firstBad, reason};\n    int out[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Allreduce(in, out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, comm);\n\n    if (out[0] == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (out[1] == 1) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        } else if (out[1] == 2) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        }\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint // number of bodies owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> displs; // first body index owned by each rank\n};\n\nstatic Partition partitionBodies(const int n, const int size) {\n    Partition p;\n    p.counts.resize(size);\n    p.displs.resize(size);\n    const int base = n / size;\n    const int rem = n % size;\n    int off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        p.counts[r] = base + (r < rem ? 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    int l = 0;\n\n    for (; l + LANES <= localN; l += LANES) {\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i; work per i shrinks with i, so\n    // the two halves of the index space are paired up for load balance.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1], pz = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        const double vx = vel[3 * l + 0], vy = vel[3 * l + 1], vz = vel[3 * l + 2];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) ||    // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, const int rank,\n                        MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int firstBad = 0, reason = 0;\n    validateLocal(pos, begin, localN, vel, firstBad, reason);\n\n    // Report the violation of the lowest-numbered body, as the serial code does.\n    int in[2] = {firstBad, reason};\n    int out[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Allreduce(in, out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, comm);\n\n    if (out[0] == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (out[1] == 1) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        } else if (out[1] == 2) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        }\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v // number of bodies owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> displs; // first body index owned by each rank\n};\n\nstatic Partition partitionBodies(const int n, const int size) {\n    Partition p;\n    p.counts.resize(size);\n    p.displs.resize(size);\n    const int base = n / size;\n    const int rem = n % size;\n    int off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        p.counts[r] = base + (r < rem ? 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    int l = 0;\n\n    for (; l + LANES <= localN; l += LANES) {\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * (begin + l + k) + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi =          const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1], pz = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        const double vx = vel[3 * l + 0], vy = vel[3 * l + 1], vz = vel[3 * l + 2];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) || !std::isfinite(pz) || !std::isfinite(vx) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy) || !std::isfinite(vz)) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 1;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const // number of bodies owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> displs; // first body index owned by each rank\n};\n\nstatic Partition partitionBodies(const int n, const int size) {\n    Partition p;\n    p.counts.resize(size);\n    p.displs.resize(size);\n    const int base = n / size;\n    const int rem = n % size;\n    int off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        p.counts[r] = base + (r < rem ? 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; l += LANES) {\n        // Lanes past the end of the local block duplicate the last body; their\n        // results are simply not stored. Keeping a single code path for all\n        // bodies makes the result independent of the block size.\n        const int m = std::min(LANES, localN - l);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            const int i = begin + l + (k < m ? k : m - 1);\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (; l < localN; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) || !std::isfinite(pz) || !std::isfinite(vx) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy) || !std::isfinite(vz)) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, const int rank,\n                        MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int firstBad = 0, reason = 0;\n    validateLocal(pos, begin, localN, vel, firstBad, reason);\n\n    // Report the violation of the lowest-numbered body, as the serial code does.\n    int in[2] = {firstBad, reason};\n    int out[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Allreduce(in, out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, comm);\n\n    if (out[0] == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (out[1] == 1) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        } else if (out[1] == 2) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        }\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n // number of bodies owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> displs; // first body index owned by each rank\n};\n\nstatic Partition partitionBodies(const int n, const int size) {\n    Partition p;\n    p.counts.resize(size);\n    p.displs.resize(size);\n    const int base = n / size;\n    const int rem = n % size;\n    int off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        p.counts[r] = base + (r < rem ? 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; l += LANES) {\n        // Lanes past the end of the local block duplicate the last body; their\n        // results are simply not stored. Keeping a single code path for all\n        // bodies makes the result independent of the block size.\n        const int m = std::min(LANES, localN - l);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            const int i = begin + l + (k < m ? k : m - 1);\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1], pz = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        const double vx = vel[3 * l + 0], vy = vel[3 * l + 1], vz = vel[3 * l + 2];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) || !std::isfinite(pz) || !std::isfinite(vx) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy) || !std::isfinite(vz)) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, const int rank,\n                        MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int firstBad = 0, reason = 0;\n    validateLocal(pos, begin, localN, vel, firstBad, reason);\n\n    // Report the violation of the lowest-numbered body, as the serial code does.\n    int in[2] = {firstBad, reason};\n    int out[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Allreduce(in, out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, comm);\n\n    if (out[0] == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (out[1] == 1) {\n            printf(\"Validation      printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        }\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s Keeping a single code path for all\n        // bodies makes the result independent of the block size.\n        const int m = std::min(LANES, localN - l);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            const int i = begin + l + (k < m ? k : m - 1);\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            // The volatile temporaries keep the compiler from fusing the\n            // multiply into the following add, matching the rounding of the\n            // scalar reference implementation. This is outside the O(n) inner\n            // loop, so it costs nothing measurable.\n            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];\n            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];\n            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += dvx;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += dvy;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += dvz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 0];\n        }\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[2 * r + 1];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, const int n, const int begin,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && 3 * l + 2 < (int)vel.size()) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; l += LANES) {\n        // Lanes past the end of the local block duplicate the last body; their\n        // results are simply not stored. Keeping a single code path for all\n        // bodies makes the result independent of the block size.\n        const int m = std::min(LANES, localN - l);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            const int i = begin + l + (k < m ? k : m - 1);\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            // The volatile temporaries keep the compiler from fusing the\n            // multiply into the following add, matching the rounding of the\n            // scalar reference implementation. This is outside the O(n) inner\n            // loop, so it costs nothing measurable.\n            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];\n            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];\n            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += dvx;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += dvy;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += dvz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // The kinetic part is summed in body order, exactly as in the serial code,\n    // and rank 0 keeps accumulating the potential terms on top of it.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> allKinetic(rank == 0 ? (size_t)size : 0);\n        MPI_Gather(&kinetic, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, allKinetic.data(), 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            kinetic = 0.0;\n            for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n                kinetic += allKinetic[r];\n            }\n        } else {\n            kinetic = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = kinetic;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const double local[2] = {kinetic, potential};\n    std::vector<double> all(2 * (size_t)size);\n    MPI_Gather(local, 2, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 2, body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n 1 : 0);\n        p.displs[r] = off;\n        off += p.counts[r];\n    }\n    return p;\n}\n\n// Positions are kept replicated (interleaved x,y,z) on every rank; velocities\n// only for the locally owned block of bodies.\nvoid randomizeBodies(double* pos, double* vel, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                     unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // The RNG stream is sequential, so every rank replays it in full and keeps\n    // the positions plus its own slice of the velocities.\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i + 0] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vx = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vy = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const double vz = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        const int l = i - begin;\n        if (l >= 0 && l < localN) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = vx;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = vy;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = vz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN,\n                   double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; l += LANES) {\n        // Lanes past the end of the local block duplicate the last body; their\n        // results are simply not stored. Keeping a single code path for all\n        // bodies makes the result independent of the block size.\n        const int m = std::min(LANES, localN - l);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES] = {}, Fy[LANES] = {}, Fz[LANES] = {};\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n            const int i = begin + l + (k < m ? k : m - 1);\n            xi[k] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[k] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[k] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (int k = 0; k < LANES; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            // The volatile temporaries keep the compiler from fusing the\n            // multiply into the following add, matching the rounding of the\n            // scalar reference implementation. This is outside the O(n) inner\n            // loop, so it costs nothing measurable.\n            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];\n            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];\n            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += dvx;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += dvy;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += dvz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // The kinetic part is summed in body order, exactly as in the serial code,\n    // and rank 0 keeps accumulating the potential terms on top of it.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> allKinetic(rank == 0 ? The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = kinetic;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> all(rank == 0 ? This is outside the O(n) inner\n            // loop, so it costs nothing measurable.\n            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];\n            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];\n            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 0] += dvx;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 1] += dvy;\n            vel[3 * (l + k) + 2] += dvz;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict__ pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* __restrict__ vel) {\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy: every rank accumulates the contributions of its own\n// block of bodies (in ascending index order, as in the serial code) and the\n// block sums are added up in rank order on rank 0.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const double* pos, const int n, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel,\n                          const int rank, const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    double kinetic = 0.0;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        kinetic += 0.5 * (vel[3 * l + 0] * vel[3 * l + 0] + vel[3 * l + 1] * vel[3 * l + 1] +\n                          vel[3 * l + 2] * vel[3 * l + 2]);\n    }\n\n    // The kinetic part is completed in body order, exactly as in the serial\n    // code, and rank 0 then keeps accumulating the potential terms on top of it.\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? (size_t)size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(&kinetic, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, partials.data(), 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n    double running = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            running += partials[r];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). The pair (i, j)\n    // with i < j is evaluated by the owner of i.\n    double potential = running;\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const int i = begin + l;\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n        for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            potential -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> all(rank == 0 ? (size_t)size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(&potential, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            energy += all[r];\n        }\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns the index of the first offending body and the kind of violation;\n// checks the locally owned bodies only.\nstatic void validateLocal(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, int& firstBad,\n                          int& reason) {\n    firstBad = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n    reason = 0;\n\n    for (int l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n        const double px = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 0], py = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 1], pz = pos[3 * (begin + l) + 2];\n        const double vx = vel[3 * l + 0], vy = vel[3 * l + 1], vz = vel[3 * l + 2];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px) || !std::isfinite(py) || !std::isfinite(pz) || !std::isfinite(vx) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy) || !std::isfinite(vz)) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 1;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px) > maxPos || std::abs(py) > maxPos || std::abs(pz) > maxPos) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 2;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx) > maxVel || std::abs(vy) > maxVel || std::abs(vz) > maxVel) {\n            firstBad = begin + l;\n            reason = 3;\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const double* pos, const int begin, const int localN, const double* vel, const int rank,\n                        MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int firstBad = 0, reason = 0;\n    validateLocal(pos, begin, localN, vel, firstBad, reason);\n\n    // Report the violation of the lowest-numbered body, as the serial code does.\n    int in[2] = {firstBad, reason};\n    int out[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Allreduce(in, out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, comm);\n\n    if (out[0] == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        return true;\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (out[1] == 1) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        } else if (out[1] == 2) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        }\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies < 0) numBodies = 0;\n    // More ranks than bodies: the surplus ranks idle in the collectives.\n    const Partition part = partitionBodies(numBodies, size);\n    const int begin = part.displs[rank];\n    const int localN = part.counts[rank];\n\n    // Communication of the replicated position array: one element per body.\n    MPI_Datatype vec3Type;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &vec3Type);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&vec3Type);\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    std::vector<double> pos((size_t)numBodies * 3);\n    std::vector<double> vel((size_t)localN * 3);\n    randomizeBodies(pos.data(), vel.data(), numBodies, begin, localN);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(pos.data(), numBodies, begin, localN, vel.data());\n        integrateBodies(pos.data(), begin, localN, vel.data());\n        // Republish the locally updated positions to all ranks.\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, localN, vec3Type, pos.data(), part.counts.data(), part.displs.data(), vec3Type,\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Collect the distributed velocities on rank 0\n        std::vector<double> allVel(rank == 0 ? (size_t)numBodies * 3 : 0);\n        MPI_Gatherv(vel.data(), localN, vec3Type, allVel.data(), part.counts.data(), part.displs.data(), vec3Type, 0,\n                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n            std::vector<double> bodyData;\n            bodyData.reserve((size_t)numBodies * 6);\n            for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n                bodyData.push_back(pos[3 * i + 0]);\n                bodyData.push_back(pos[3 * i + 1]);\n                bodyData.push_back(pos[3 * i + 2]);\n                bodyData.push_back(allVel[3 * i + 0]);\n                bodyData.push_back(allVel[3 * i + 1]);\n                bodyData.push_back(allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n            }\n            print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    int status = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n        }\n\n        if (validateSimulation(pos.data(), begin, localN, vel.data(), rank, MPI_COMM_WORLD)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy =\n                computeTotalEnergy(pos.data(), numBodies, begin, localN, vel.data(), rank, size, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            }\n            status = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Type_free(&vec3Type);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return status;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":163,"oldLines":13,"newStart":163,"newLines":12,"lines":["         }","     }"," ","-    std::vector<double> all(rank == 0 ? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CehoUYguNfkigKHefqWd8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The nbody benchmark now runs distributed under MPI.\n\n**Design** (`nbody/nbody.cpp`):\n- **Decomposition**: bodies split into contiguous blocks (first `n % size` ranks get one extra). Positions are replicated on every rank (interleaved `x,y,z`); velocities are stored only for locally owned bodies.\n- **Per step**: each rank computes forces for its own bodies against all `n` positions, integrates its own positions, then republishes them with a single `MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, …)` using a committed 3-double datatype (one message per step, minimum possible volume).\n- **Init**: every rank replays the identical `rand_r` stream (O(n), no communication) and keeps its slice — no scatter needed.\n- **Kernel**: the force loop interleaves `LANES=8` bodies with independent accumulators so GCC emits `vsqrtpd`/`vdivpd`/`vfmadd…pd` (256-bit). Each accumulator still sums `j` in ascending order, so rounding is unchanged; tail lanes duplicate the last body and are not stored, keeping one code path for any block size. This alone makes a single rank ~4× faster than the original scalar build, and the kernel is now at the hardware divide/sqrt throughput limit.\n- **Validation/energy/results**: `-r` gathers velocities to rank 0 and prints in original body order; `-v` checks bodies locally and reports the lowest-numbered violation via `MPI_2INT/MPI_MINLOC`; energy is computed in parallel with rank 0 accumulating block partials in rank order (kinetic total first, then potential, matching the serial accumulation chain). All printing is rank-0 only.\n\n**Correctness**: output is byte-identical to the original for every rank count tested (1–128) — e.g. `diff` of `-n 2048 -s 20 -v -r` at 64 ranks vs. the original binary differs only in the timing line, including the `Final energy` digits. Verified across `n = 0, 3, 1024, 4096, 16384, 65536` and non-divisible rank counts (3, 5, 7, 13, 33), plus `-h`/unknown-option paths and `np > n`.\n\nOne deliberate deviation: reproducing the original's rounding required blocking FMA contraction in the velocity update (`vel += DT*F`), which GCC fuses in vectorized code but not in the scalar original — done with `volatile` temporaries outside the O(n) inner loop, so it has no measurable cost.\n\n**Scaling** (dual EPYC 7763, 128 cores, `--bind-to core`):\n\n| ranks | n=16384, 10 steps | n=65536, 5 steps |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 5044 ms (original: ~20 s) | — |\n| 16 | 321 ms | 2563 ms |\n| 64 | 109 ms | 696 ms |\n| 128 | 75 ms | 387 ms |\n\nThat's 67× over the MPI version's own single rank at n=16384 (~265× over the original serial code), and 83% efficiency from 16→128 ranks at n=65536, where the allgather is fully amortized.\n\n`nbody/CMakeLists.txt` adds `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`; executable name and flags are unchanged, no new files or dependencies."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":343,"cache_read_input_tokens":85043,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":343},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"840ae13f-e992-4906-9575-4044c52b2020","uuid":"ec9dab19-4971-4e9f-aed0-2f4d6b8a5d22","timestamp":"2026-09-04T04:54:32.435Z","request_id":"req_011CehoUXnaWKyvNKZo9KEgE"}
